/* Phone-only navigation; original SAP records, merge keys and persistence stay intact. */
(() => {
  'use strict';
  const mq = window.matchMedia('(max-width:1024px)');
  const byId = id => document.getElementById(id);
  const app = document.querySelector('.app');
  const cards = byId('cards'), filters = byId('workingFilterToolbar');
  const tools = byId('systemTools'), searchInput = byId('mobileSearch');
  const filterButton = byId('mobileFilterToggle'), toast = byId('mobileToast');
  const workspace = byId('mobileWorkspace'), bottomNav = byId('mobileBottomNav');
  const filterIds = ['workingPriority','workingDepartment','workingDateFrom','workingDateTo'];
  const tickerTitle = document.querySelector('.permit-ticker-title');
  const originalTickerText = tickerTitle.textContent;
  let pageName='home', lastRecordKey='', searchTimer, toastTimer, restoring=false, resetPanes=false;
  let wasMobile=mq.matches, detailReturnKey='';
  const priorityFoldTimers=new Map(), foldedPriorityCards=new Map();
  const PRIORITY_FOLD_DELAY=220;
  let pressedWorkPriority=null;
  let foldContentCounter=0, permitTickerSignature=null;
  let tickerIsPaused=window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)').matches;

  const titles={screens:'בחירת מסך',search:'חיפוש הודעות',departments:'מחלקות',tools:'ניהול מערכת'};
  const icons={rows:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="16" rx="3"/><path d="M3 9h18M3 14h18M9 4v16"/></svg>',cards:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="7" rx="2"/><rect x="3" y="14" width="18" height="6" rx="2"/></svg>',arrow:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m14 7-5 5 5 5"/></svg>'};

  function message(text) {
    if (!mq.matches) return;
    toast.textContent=text; toast.classList.add('visible');
    clearTimeout(toastTimer); toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>toast.classList.remove('visible'),2600);
  }
  // Some Android document viewers do not allow persistent localStorage.
  const fallback=new Map(); let volatileStorage=false, fallbackUi=null;
  const baseGet=Store.get.bind(Store), baseSet=Store.set.bind(Store);
  const baseUi=Store.ui.bind(Store), baseSetUi=Store.setUi.bind(Store);
  Store.get=k=>fallback.has(k)?fallback.get(k):baseGet(k);
  Store.set=(k,patch)=>{
    try {const saved=baseSet(k,patch);fallback.delete(k);return saved;}
    catch {const saved={...Store.get(k),...patch,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()};fallback.set(k,saved);volatileStorage=true;return saved;}
  };
  Store.ui=()=>fallbackUi||baseUi();
  Store.setUi=patch=>{try {baseSetUi(patch);} catch {fallbackUi={...Store.ui(),...patch};}};
  const screenMode=()=>Store.ui().mobileScreen==='maintenance'?'maintenance':'operations';
  let prioritySummaryMode=false;
  const isUrgentRecord=r=>!!r&&isUrgentPriorityValue(priorityValue(r));
  const visibleUrgentRows=()=>SAP.filter(r=>(!window.TOVATI_TEST_ACCESS||window.TOVATI_TEST_ACCESS.raw(r))&&isUrgentRecord(r)&&!isClosed(r));
  function prioritySummaryRows(){
    const map=new Map();
    for(const r of visibleUrgentRows()){
      const code=maintenanceDepartment(r)||'UNASSIGNED',entry=map.get(code)||{code,total:0,prioritized:0,immediate:0,today:0,tomorrow:0,night:0,pending:0};
      const op=manual(r).operational||'';entry.total++;if(op){entry.prioritized++;if(Object.hasOwn(entry,op))entry[op]++;}else entry.pending++;map.set(code,entry);
    }
    return [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>{const ai=MAINT_DEPT_ORDER.indexOf(a.code),bi=MAINT_DEPT_ORDER.indexOf(b.code);return(ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi)||a.code.localeCompare(b.code,'he',{numeric:true});});
  }
  function prioritySummaryHTML(){
    const rows=prioritySummaryRows(),all=rows.reduce((s,x)=>({total:s.total+x.total,prioritized:s.prioritized+x.prioritized,pending:s.pending+x.pending}),{total:0,prioritized:0,pending:0});
    return `<section class="priority-session-summary r15-priority-summary"><header class="r15-priority-header"><strong>סיכום תעדוף</strong><span><b>${number(all.prioritized)}</b> / ${number(all.total)} תועדפו</span></header><div class="r15-priority-table-scroll"><table class="r15-priority-table"><thead><tr><th>מחלקה</th><th>מיידי</th><th>להיום</th><th>למחר</th><th>ממתין</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(x=>`<tr><th><bdi>${esc(x.code)}</bdi> ${esc(deptName(x.code))}</th><td class="is-immediate">${number(x.immediate)}</td><td class="is-today">${number(x.today)}</td><td class="is-tomorrow">${number(x.tomorrow)}</td><td>${number(x.pending)}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div><small>${number(all.pending)} עבודות עדיין ממתינות לתעדוף</small></section>`;
  }
  function setPrioritySummaryMode(on){prioritySummaryMode=!!on;document.body.classList.toggle('priority-summary-mode',prioritySummaryMode);draw();syncPrioritySessionBar();if(prioritySummaryMode)requestAnimationFrame(()=>cards.scrollIntoView({block:'start',behavior:'smooth'}));}
  function writePriorityRaw(r,value=''){
    const k=key(r);if(!k)return;
    try{baseSet(k,{operational:value});fallback.delete(k);}catch{const saved={...Store.get(k),operational:value,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()};fallback.set(k,saved);volatileStorage=true;}
  }
  function resetAllPriorities(){
    const prioritized=SAP.filter(r=>(manual(r).operational||''));
    prioritized.forEach(r=>writePriorityRaw(r,''));
    priorityFoldTimers.forEach((_,id)=>cancelPriorityFold(id));foldedPriorityCards.clear();prioritySummaryMode=false;document.body.classList.remove('priority-summary-mode');
    update();syncPrioritySessionBar();message(prioritized.length?`אופסו ${number(prioritized.length)} תעדופים`:'אין תעדופים לאיפוס');
  }
  function ensurePrioritySessionBar(){
    let bar=byId('prioritySessionBar');if(bar){if(bar.parentElement!==document.querySelector('.app'))document.querySelector('.app').append(bar);return bar;}
    bar=document.createElement('div');bar.id='prioritySessionBar';bar.className='priority-session-bar';bar.innerHTML='<button type="button" data-priority-reset>איפוס</button><button type="button" class="primary" data-priority-finish>סיימתי תעדוף</button>';
    document.querySelector('.app').append(bar);
    bar.addEventListener('click',e=>{const reset=e.target.closest('[data-priority-reset]'),finish=e.target.closest('[data-priority-finish]');if(reset){if(confirm('לאפס את כל התעדופים שנבחרו?'))resetAllPriorities();return;}if(finish){if(prioritySummaryMode){setPrioritySummaryMode(false);return;}try{const receipt=window.TOVATI_BRIEFING.publish();setPrioritySummaryMode(true);document.querySelector('.priority-session-summary')?.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin','<div class="r12-publish-receipt"><span>סיכומי המחלקות נשמרו בפיד במכשיר זה</span><button type="button" data-p-open="feed">פתח את הפיד ←</button></div>');message(receipt.changed?'סיכום התעדוף פורסם בפיד המחלקות במכשיר זה.':'הסיכום כבר מעודכן בפיד — לא נוצרו כפילויות.');}catch(err){message(err.message);}}});
    return bar;
  }
  let priorityEligibleKeys=new Set(),prioritySelectedKeys=new Set();
  function syncPriorityProgress(recordKey,selected){
    if(priorityEligibleKeys.has(recordKey)){selected?prioritySelectedKeys.add(recordKey):prioritySelectedKeys.delete(recordKey);}
    const bar=byId('prioritySessionBar');if(bar)bar.dataset.progress=`${prioritySelectedKeys.size}/${priorityEligibleKeys.size}`;
  }
  function syncPrioritySessionBar(){
    const bar=ensurePrioritySessionBar(),show=mq.matches&&pageName==='home'&&screenMode()==='operations'&&!document.body.classList.contains('row-detail-open')&&!document.body.classList.contains('pilot-open');bar.hidden=!show;
    const finish=bar.querySelector('[data-priority-finish]');if(finish)finish.textContent=prioritySummaryMode?'חזרה לתעדוף':'סיימתי תעדוף';
    const rows=visibleUrgentRows();priorityEligibleKeys=new Set(rows.map(key));prioritySelectedKeys=new Set(rows.filter(r=>manual(r).operational).map(key));bar.dataset.progress=`${prioritySelectedKeys.size}/${priorityEligibleKeys.size}`;
  }
  const mobileScreenButtons=()=>Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-mobile-screen]'));
  function applyScreenMode() {
    const maintenance=mq.matches&&screenMode()==='maintenance';
    if(maintenance||!mq.matches)priorityFoldTimers.forEach((pending,id)=>cancelPriorityFold(id));
    document.body.classList.toggle('mobile-screen-maintenance',maintenance);
    document.body.classList.toggle('mobile-screen-operations',mq.matches&&!maintenance);
    if(maintenance&&prioritySummaryMode){prioritySummaryMode=false;document.body.classList.remove('priority-summary-mode');}
    mobileScreenButtons().forEach(button=>{const chosen=button.dataset.mobileScreen===screenMode();button.classList.toggle('active',chosen);button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(chosen));});
  }
  function deptName(code){return MAINT_DEPT_NAMES[code]||('מחלקה '+(code==='UNASSIGNED'?'לא משויכת':code));}
  function groupsOf(rows){const map=new Map();rows.forEach(r=>{const code=maintenanceDepartment(r)||'UNASSIGNED';map.set(code,(map.get(code)||0)+1);});return map;}
  function sortedCodes(groups){return [...groups.keys()].sort((a,b)=>{const ai=MAINT_DEPT_ORDER.indexOf(a),bi=MAINT_DEPT_ORDER.indexOf(b);return (ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi)||a.localeCompare(b,'he',{numeric:true});});}
  const number=n=>Number(n).toLocaleString('he-IL');
  let reportSourceRefs=[],reportUserKey='',reportGeneratedAt='';
  
function renderSummary() {
  if(!mq.matches)return;
  const viewer=window.TOVATI_TEST?.current?.(),maint=screenMode()==='maintenance';
  const refs=[DATASETS.notifications,DATASETS.orders,DATASETS.permits],userKey=(viewer?.id||'admin')+'|'+screenMode();
  if(!reportGeneratedAt||userKey!==reportUserKey||refs.some((x,i)=>x!==reportSourceRefs[i])){
    reportSourceRefs=refs;reportUserKey=userKey;reportGeneratedAt=new Date().toISOString();
  }
  const allowed=SAP.filter(r=>!window.TOVATI_TEST_ACCESS||window.TOVATI_TEST_ACCESS.raw(r));
  const selected=byId('workingDepartment')?.value||'',ownDept=viewer?.branch==='maintenance'?viewer.dept:'',dept=maint?(ownDept||selected):'';
  const rows=maint&&dept?allowed.filter(r=>maintenanceDepartment(r)===dept):allowed;
  const sectionGroups=new Map(),departments=groupsOf(rows);

  const sectionItem=g=>{
    const lookup=window.TOVATI_USERS?.groups?.().find(x=>x.type==='section'&&x.code===g.code&&x.dept===g.dept),name=lookup?.name;
    return `<div class="summary-department summary-section" data-section="${esc(g.code)}"><span><strong>${esc(g.code||'ללא מדור')}</strong>${name&&name!==g.code?`<small>${esc(name)}</small>`:''}</span><b>${number(g.count)}</b></div>`;
  };

  let summaryBody='';
  if(maint){
    for(const r of rows){
      const d=maintenanceDepartment(r)||'UNASSIGNED',code=String(center(r)||'').trim().replace(/^3500\//,''),k=d+'|'+code;
      if(!sectionGroups.has(k))sectionGroups.set(k,{dept:d,code:code||'ללא מדור',count:0});
      sectionGroups.get(k).count++;
    }
    const sorted=[...sectionGroups.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.count-a.count||a.dept.localeCompare(b.dept,'he',{numeric:true})||a.code.localeCompare(b.code,'he',{numeric:true}));
    summaryBody=`<div class="r11-summary-caption">${esc(dept?deptName(dept)+' · לפי מדורים':'לפי מדורים במחלקות המוצגות')}</div><div class="summary-departments">${sorted.slice(0,6).map(sectionItem).join('')}</div>${sorted.length>6?`<details class="r10-summary-more"><summary>כל המדורים · ${number(sorted.length)}</summary><div class="summary-departments">${sorted.slice(6).map(sectionItem).join('')}</div></details>`:''}`;
  }else{
    summaryBody=`<div class="summary-departments r11-department-summary">${sortedCodes(departments).map(code=>`<div class="summary-department" data-summary-dept="${esc(code)}"><span>${esc(deptName(code))}</span><b>${number(departments.get(code))}</b></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
  }

  const at=new Date(reportGeneratedAt),date=at.toLocaleDateString('he-IL',{day:'2-digit',month:'2-digit',year:'numeric'}),time=at.toLocaleTimeString('he-IL',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',second:'2-digit',hour12:false});
  byId('mobileSummaryBox').dataset.summaryMode=maint?'sections':'departments';

  const pcounts={'חירום':0,'דחוף':0,'רגיל':0,'לא דחוף':0,'הפסקת יחידה':0};
  for(const r of rows){
    const p=priorityValue(r);
    pcounts[p]=(pcounts[p]||0)+1;
  }
  const labels={'חירום':'1 · חירום','דחוף':'2 · דחוף','רגיל':'3 · רגיל','לא דחוף':'4 · לא דחוף','הפסקת יחידה':'5–6 · הפסקת יחידה'};
  const order=['חירום','דחוף','רגיל','לא דחוף','הפסקת יחידה'];
  const urgentCount=(pcounts['חירום']||0)+(pcounts['דחוף']||0),urgentPercent=rows.length?(100*urgentCount/rows.length):0;
  const stats=`<div class="r15-priority-stats">${order.map(k=>`<div class="r15-stat ${['חירום','דחוף'].includes(k)?'r15-stat-urgent':k==='הפסקת יחידה'?'r15-stat-outage':''}"><span>${esc(labels[k])}</span><b>${number(pcounts[k]||0)}</b></div>`).join('')}</div><div class="r15-urgent-percent"><div><strong>${urgentPercent.toFixed(1)}%</strong><span>דחופות וחירום מכל העבודות בדוח</span></div><small>${number(urgentCount)} מתוך ${number(rows.length)} · לא כולל מאגר תחזוקה מונעת</small></div>`;

  byId('mobileSummaryBox').dataset.r15ReportKey=userKey+'|'+reportGeneratedAt;
  byId('mobileSummaryBox').innerHTML=`<div class="summary-topline r15-report-top r24-classic-report-top"><div class="r15-title-group"><div class="summary-title">דוח יומי לתעדוף עבודות</div><div class="r11-report-stamp" title="מועד הפקת התצוגה במכשיר"><i class="r11-report-light" aria-hidden="true"></i><time datetime="${esc(reportGeneratedAt)}"><bdi>${esc(date)} · ${esc(time)}</bdi></time></div></div><div class="summary-total"><strong>${number(rows.length)}</strong><small>פקודות</small></div><button type="button" class="r15-summary-toggle" aria-label="קיפול הדוח" aria-expanded="true" aria-controls="r15ReportBody" data-r15-action="toggle-report"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="m7 14 5-5 5 5"/></svg></button></div><div id="r15ReportBody" class="r15-report-body r24-classic-report-body">${summaryBody}${stats}${state.rows.length!==rows.length?`<div class="summary-filter-note"><span>לאחר חיפוש וסינון</span><b>${number(state.rows.length)} מתוך ${number(rows.length)}</b></div>`:''}</div>`;
  byId('mobileSummaryBox').dataset.r15Counts=JSON.stringify(pcounts);
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('tovati:summary-rendered'));
  window.TOVATI_DAILY_REPORT={generatedAt:reportGeneratedAt,groupBy:maint?'section':'department',department:dept,total:rows.length,groups:maint?[...sectionGroups.values()]:sortedCodes(departments).map(code=>({code,count:departments.get(code)}))};
}
  function renderDepartments(){
    const totals=groupsOf(SAP.filter(r=>!window.TOVATI_TEST_ACCESS||window.TOVATI_TEST_ACCESS.raw(r))), selected=byId('workingDepartment').value;
    byId('mobileDeptList').innerHTML=`<button type="button" class="${selected?'':'active'}" data-department-choice="" aria-pressed="${!selected}"><span>כל המחלקות<small>כל ההודעות שבקובץ</small></span><b class="dept-number">${number(SAP.filter(r=>!window.TOVATI_TEST_ACCESS||window.TOVATI_TEST_ACCESS.raw(r)).length)}</b></button>`+sortedCodes(totals).map(code=>`<button type="button" class="${selected===code?'active':''}" data-department-choice="${esc(code)}" aria-pressed="${selected===code}"><span>${esc(deptName(code))}</span><b class="dept-number">${number(totals.get(code))}</b></button>`).join('');
  }
  function renderSearch(){
    const rows=state.rows;
    byId('mobileSearchCount').textContent=`${number(rows.length)} הודעות${filterIds.some(id=>byId(id).value)?' · לפי הסינון הפעיל':''}`;
    byId('mobileSearchResults').innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(r=>`<button type="button" class="search-result" data-open-work="${esc(key(r))}"><span class="result-heading"><bdi>${esc(notificationNo(r))}</bdi><span>${esc(maintenanceDepartmentName(r))}</span></span><strong>${esc(description(r)||'ללא תיאור')}</strong><span class="result-ids">הזמנה: <bdi>${esc(orderNo(r)||'לא קיימת')}</bdi> · היתר: <bdi>${esc(permitNo(r)||'לא קיים')}</bdi></span></button>`).join(''):'<div class="workspace-empty">לא נמצאו הודעות מתאימות.<br>אפשר לשנות את החיפוש או לנקות את הסינון.</div>';
  }
  function refreshTools(){
    const outside=DATASETS.permits.filter(p=>String(val(p,ALIASES.permitStatus)||'').trim()==='מחוץ').length;
    const requested=DATASETS.permits.filter(p=>String(val(p,ALIASES.permitStatus)||'').trim()==='בקשה').length;
    byId('mobileDataNote').innerHTML=`<strong>הנתונים בקובץ</strong><br>${number(DATASETS.notifications.length)} הודעות · ${number(DATASETS.orders.length)} הזמנות · ${number(DATASETS.permits.length)} היתרים<br>היתרים: ${number(outside)} מחוץ · ${number(requested)} בקשה<br>עדכונים ידניים נשמרים בדפדפן במכשיר הזה. אין סנכרון אוטומטי בין מכשירים.`;
  }

  function renderPermitStrip(){
    const ticker=byId('permitTicker');if(!ticker)return;
    const wanted=screenMode()==='maintenance'?'מחוץ':'בקשה';
    const rows=DATASETS.permits.filter(p=>String(val(p,ALIASES.permitStatus)||'').trim()===wanted&&(!window.TOVATI_WORKSPACE||window.TOVATI_WORKSPACE.permitVisible(p))).map(p=>({
      status:wanted,permit:String(val(p,ALIASES.permit)||'').trim(),description:String(val(p,['תיאור היתר','תאור היתר',...ALIASES.description])||'').trim()
    }));
    const signature=wanted+JSON.stringify(rows);if(signature===permitTickerSignature)return;permitTickerSignature=signature;
    tickerTitle.textContent='';tickerTitle.hidden=true;tickerTitle.remove();ticker.querySelector('.permit-ticker-pause')?.remove();
    ticker.setAttribute('aria-label',`${rows.length} היתרים בסטטוס ${wanted}`);ticker.dataset.status=wanted;
    const win=ticker.querySelector('.permit-ticker-window');win.setAttribute('tabindex','0');win.setAttribute('aria-label','היתרים '+wanted+' — סטטוס, מספר ותיאור');
    const track=document.createElement('div');track.className='permit-ticker-track';
    if(rows.length){const group=document.createElement('div');group.className='permit-ticker-group';
      group.innerHTML=rows.map(p=>`<span class="permit-ticker-item ${wanted==='מחוץ'?'ticker-outside':'ticker-request'}"><b class="r10-permit-status">${esc(p.status)}</b><bdi class="r10-permit-number">${esc(p.permit||'—')}</bdi><span class="r10-permit-description">${esc(p.description||'ללא תיאור')}</span></span>`).join('');
      const copy=group.cloneNode(true);copy.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');track.append(group,copy);win.replaceChildren(track);
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(group.isConnected){const width=group.getBoundingClientRect().width;track.style.setProperty('--permit-run-time',Math.max(18,width/38)+'s');}});
    }else{const empty=document.createElement('span');empty.className='permit-ticker-empty';empty.textContent='אין היתרים בסטטוס '+wanted+' בתחום התצוגה';track.append(empty);win.replaceChildren(track);}
    ticker.classList.toggle('ticker-empty',!rows.length);ticker.classList.remove('ticker-paused');
    ticker.classList.add('ticker-motion-enabled');
  }
  function updateTickerPause(){} // The visible pause control was removed; reduced-motion remains supported.

  function feedCardsFor(recordKey){
    return Array.from(cards.querySelectorAll('.department-box .work-card[data-key]')).filter(card=>card.dataset.key===recordKey);
  }
  function currentPriority(recordKey){
    const r=workCardForKey(recordKey);
    return r?(manual(r).operational||''):'';
  }
  function cancelPriorityFold(recordKey){
    const pending=priorityFoldTimers.get(recordKey);
    if(pending)clearTimeout(pending.timer);
    priorityFoldTimers.delete(recordKey);
  }
  function syncPriorityFold(card){
    if(!mq.matches||screenMode()!=='operations'||card.closest('.row-detail-dialog')){
      card.classList.remove('priority-folded');
      card.querySelector(':scope > .priority-collapsed-row')?.remove();
      return;
    }
    const recordKey=card.dataset.key;
    let button=card.querySelector(':scope > .priority-collapsed-row');
    if(!button){
      const r=SAP.find(row=>key(row)===recordKey);
      const text=r?(description(r)||'ללא תיאור עבודה'):(card.querySelector('.desc')?.textContent||'ללא תיאור עבודה');
      button=document.createElement('button');button.type='button';button.className='priority-collapsed-row';
      button.dataset.expandPriority=recordKey;button.title=text;
      button.setAttribute('aria-label','פתיחת כרטיסיית העבודה: '+text);
      const label=document.createElement('span');label.textContent=text;button.append(label);
      const stack=card.querySelector('.card-pane-stack');
      if(stack){if(!stack.id)stack.id='priority-fold-content-'+(++foldContentCounter);button.setAttribute('aria-controls',stack.id);}
      card.append(button);
    }
    const foldedAs=foldedPriorityCards.get(recordKey);
    if(foldedAs&&currentPriority(recordKey)!==foldedAs)foldedPriorityCards.delete(recordKey);
    const folded=!!foldedPriorityCards.get(recordKey);
    card.classList.toggle('priority-folded',folded);card.dataset.priorityFolded=folded?'1':'0';
    button.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!folded));
  }
  function keepFeedPosition(change,preferred){
    const canAnchor=pageName==='home'&&!document.body.classList.contains('row-detail-open');
    let anchor=null,top=0;
    if(canAnchor){
      const rect=preferred?.getBoundingClientRect();
      if(rect&&rect.bottom>0&&rect.top<innerHeight-68)anchor=preferred;
      else anchor=Array.from(cards.querySelectorAll('.department-box .work-card')).find(card=>{const r=card.getBoundingClientRect();return r.bottom>0&&r.top<innerHeight-68;});
      if(anchor)top=Math.max(8,anchor.getBoundingClientRect().top);
    }
    change();
    if(anchor?.isConnected){const delta=anchor.getBoundingClientRect().top-top;if(Math.abs(delta)>1)window.scrollBy({top:delta,behavior:'instant'});}
  }
  function patchPriority(recordKey,selected){
    if(!mq.matches||state.viewMode!=='cards'||prioritySummaryMode)return false;
    const matches=Array.from(cards.querySelectorAll('.work-card[data-key]')).filter(c=>c.dataset.key===recordKey);
    if(!matches.length)return false;
    for(const card of matches){
      for(const op of ['immediate','today','tomorrow','night'])card.classList.toggle(op,op===selected);
      card.querySelectorAll('[data-op]').forEach(b=>{const on=b.dataset.op===selected;b.classList.toggle('selected',on);b.classList.toggle('active',on);b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(on));});
      card.querySelectorAll('[data-manual="operational"]').forEach(el=>{el.value=selected;});
      // Keep the treatment tab current without disturbing its other fields or focus.
      card.querySelectorAll('label').forEach(label=>{if(label.textContent.trim()==='תעדוף נוכחי'&&label.nextElementSibling)label.nextElementSibling.textContent=operationalText(selected);});
    }
    refreshTrafficTimers(true,recordKey);
    syncPriorityProgress(recordKey,selected);
    return true;
  }
  function queuePriorityFold(recordKey,selected,deadline=Date.now()+PRIORITY_FOLD_DELAY){
    cancelPriorityFold(recordKey);foldedPriorityCards.delete(recordKey);
    feedCardsFor(recordKey).forEach(syncPriorityFold);
    if(!selected||!mq.matches||screenMode()!=='operations'||!feedCardsFor(recordKey).length)return; // Clear, maintenance and table views never schedule a fold.
    const pending={selected,timer:null};
    pending.timer=setTimeout(()=>{
      if(priorityFoldTimers.get(recordKey)!==pending)return;
      priorityFoldTimers.delete(recordKey);
      if(!mq.matches||screenMode()!=='operations'||currentPriority(recordKey)!==selected)return;
      const matches=feedCardsFor(recordKey);
      keepFeedPosition(()=>{
        foldedPriorityCards.set(recordKey,selected);
        matches.forEach(card=>{
          // Preserve any typing before hiding the form; do not discard unsaved input.
          const active=document.activeElement,focused=card.contains(active);
          if(focused&&active?.matches('textarea[data-manual],input[data-manual]')){clearTimeout(active._t);saveFrom(active);}
          setNotesOpen(card,false);syncPriorityFold(card);card.classList.add('priority-folded');card.dataset.priorityFolded='1';
          if(focused)card.querySelector('.priority-collapsed-row')?.focus({preventScroll:true});
        });
      },matches[0]);
    },Math.max(0,deadline-Date.now()));
    priorityFoldTimers.set(recordKey,pending);
  }
  function expandPriorityCard(recordKey){
    if(!mq.matches||screenMode()!=='operations')return;
    cancelPriorityFold(recordKey);foldedPriorityCards.delete(recordKey);
    const matches=feedCardsFor(recordKey);
    keepFeedPosition(()=>matches.forEach(syncPriorityFold),matches[0]);
    lastRecordKey=recordKey;
    // It stays expanded until another priority is selected.
    matches[0]?.querySelector('.card-tab.active')?.focus({preventScroll:true});
  }

  function syncChrome(){
    if (!mq.matches) return;
    applyScreenMode(); renderSummary(); renderPermitStrip();
    const activeCount=filterIds.filter(id=>byId(id).value).length+(byId('search').value.trim()?1:0);
    filterButton.classList.toggle('has-filters',activeCount>0);
    byId('mobileFilterLabel').textContent=activeCount?`סינון (${activeCount})`:'סינון';
    byId('mobileResultCount').textContent=number(state.rows.length)+' הודעות';
    const rowsMode=state.viewMode==='table';
    byId('rowViewTop').innerHTML=(rowsMode?icons.cards:icons.rows)+`<span>${rowsMode?'כרטיסיות':'תצוגת שורות'}</span>`;
    byId('rowViewTop').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(rowsMode));
    byId('rowViewTop').setAttribute('aria-label',rowsMode?'מעבר לתצוגת כרטיסיות':'מעבר לתצוגת שורות');
    byId('mobileClearSearch').hidden=!searchInput.value;
    const oldStatus=byId('mobileSourceStatus');
    if (oldStatus) oldStatus.textContent='עדכונים ידניים נשמרים בדפדפן במכשיר הזה.';
    if(pageName==='search')renderSearch();
    if(pageName==='departments')renderDepartments();
    if(pageName==='tools')refreshTools();
    const empty=cards.querySelector('.empty');
    if(empty&&SAP.length){
      empty.innerHTML='<h3>לא נמצאו הודעות מתאימות</h3><p>אפשר לשנות את החיפוש או לנקות את הסינון.</p><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-mobile-reset="1">ניקוי חיפוש וסינון</button>';
    }
  }
  function closeTools(){tools.open=false;document.body.classList.remove('mobile-menu-open');tools.querySelector('summary').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');}
  function setFilters(open){filters.classList.toggle('mobile-filters-open',open);filterButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(open));}
  function showPage(name='home'){
    if(!mq.matches)return;
    pageName=Object.hasOwn(titles,name)?name:'home';
    closeTools();setFilters(false);
    workspace.hidden=pageName==='home';
    document.body.classList.toggle('mobile-panel-open',pageName!=='home');
    bottomNav.querySelectorAll('[data-mobile-page]').forEach(button=>{
      const active=button.dataset.mobilePage===pageName;
      button.classList.toggle('active',active);
      if(active)button.setAttribute('aria-current','page');else button.removeAttribute('aria-current');
    });
    workspace.querySelectorAll('[data-workspace-pane]').forEach(pane=>pane.hidden=pane.dataset.workspacePane!==pageName);
    if(pageName!=='home'){
      byId('mobileWorkspaceTitle').textContent=titles[pageName];
      workspace.querySelector('.workspace-scroll').scrollTop=0;
      if(pageName==='search'){searchInput.value=byId('search').value;renderSearch();byId('mobileClearSearch').hidden=!searchInput.value;}
      if(pageName==='departments')renderDepartments();
      if(pageName==='screens')applyScreenMode();
      if(pageName==='tools')refreshTools();
    }
    syncPrioritySessionBar();
  }
  function setNotesOpen(card,open){
    const toggle=card.querySelector('[data-toggle-ops-notes]');
    const panel=card.querySelector('.ops-notes-panel');
    if(!toggle||!panel)return;
    if(!open&&!panel.hidden){
      const field=panel.querySelector('textarea[data-manual="note"]');
      if(field){clearTimeout(field._t);const row=workCardForKey(card.dataset.key);if(row&&field.value!==(manual(row).note||''))saveFrom(field);}
    }
    toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(open));
    panel.hidden=!open;
    // R5 replaced this text with a chevron. Older/custom layouts may still have it.
    const actionLabel=toggle.querySelector('.ops-note-action-label');
    if(actionLabel)actionLabel.textContent=open?'סגירה':'פתיחה';
    toggle.title=open?'סגירת הערות':'פתיחת הערות';
  }
  function enhanceCards(root=cards){
    if(!mq.matches)return;
    root.querySelectorAll('.work-card.tabbed-work-card').forEach(card=>{
      const preview=false; // R10: maintenance uses the same tabbed cards as operations.
      card.querySelectorAll('[data-tab]').forEach(tab=>{
        tab.type='button';if(preview)tab.classList.toggle('active',tab.dataset.tab==='general');
        tab.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(tab.classList.contains('active')));
      });
      if(preview)card.querySelectorAll('[data-pane]').forEach(pane=>pane.classList.toggle('hidden',pane.dataset.pane!=='general'));
      card.querySelectorAll('[data-op]').forEach(button=>{button.type='button';button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(button.classList.contains('selected')));if(button.dataset.op==='night')button.remove();});
      card.querySelectorAll('[data-manual="operational"]').forEach(select=>{select.disabled=screenMode()==='maintenance';select.classList.add('work-priority-editor');if(select.previousElementSibling?.tagName==='LABEL')select.previousElementSibling.classList.add('work-priority-editor');select.querySelectorAll('option[value="night"]').forEach(option=>{if(option.selected){option.disabled=true;}else option.remove();});});
      syncPriorityFold(card);
      if(card.dataset.mobileReady==='1')return;
      card.dataset.mobileReady='1';
      const summary=card.querySelector('.general-pane .summary'), grid=summary?.querySelector('.summary-grid');
      if(grid){
        const fields=Array.from(grid.children), primary=fields.filter(field=>['סטאטוס הזמנה','סטטוס היתר'].includes(field.querySelector('b')?.textContent.trim()));
        if(primary.length){
          // Keep each status inside its existing number box, with no extra title.
          primary.forEach(field=>{
            const statusLabel=field.querySelector('b')?.textContent.trim();
            const numberLabel=statusLabel==='סטאטוס הזמנה'?'מספר הזמנה':'מספר היתר';
            const target=Array.from(card.querySelectorAll('.general-pane .card-head .ids .id'))
              .find(box=>box.querySelector('b')?.textContent.trim()===numberLabel);
            if(!target)return; // Preserve the field if a custom layout has no matching number box.
            const valueNode=field.cloneNode(true);
            valueNode.querySelector('b')?.remove();
            const value=valueNode.textContent.trim();
            if(value&&value!=='המידע אינו קיים בקובץ'){
              const statusClass=statusLabel==='סטאטוס הזמנה'?'order-user-status':'';
              if(!statusClass||!target.querySelector('.'+statusClass)){
                const status=document.createElement('span');
                status.className=('id-status '+statusClass).trim();status.dir='rtl';status.textContent=value;
                status.setAttribute('aria-label',statusLabel+': '+value);
                target.append(status);
              }
            }
            field.remove();
          });
          if(grid && !grid.children.length)grid.remove();
        }
      }
      if(summary){
        // Remove only these three presentation boxes; underlying report data is untouched.
        const removedLabels=new Set(['מיקום פונקציונלי','סטאטוס / חסם','מצב ביצוע מחושב']);
        summary.querySelectorAll('.summary-grid .datum').forEach(field=>{
          if(removedLabels.has(field.querySelector('b')?.textContent.trim()))field.remove();
        });
        summary.querySelectorAll('.summary-grid').forEach(group=>{if(!group.children.length)group.remove();});
        const record=SAP.find(row=>key(row)===card.dataset.key);
        const meta=document.createElement('div');meta.className='operations-meta-row';
        const panelId='ops-note-'+card.dataset.key.replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/ig,'-')+(card.closest('.row-detail-dialog')?'-dialog':'-card');
        const noteButton=document.createElement('button');noteButton.type='button';noteButton.className='ops-notes-toggle';
        noteButton.dataset.toggleOpsNotes='1';noteButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');noteButton.setAttribute('aria-controls',panelId);
        noteButton.innerHTML='<span class="ops-note-title">הערות טכנאי התפעול</span><span class="ops-note-action" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="m8 10 4 4 4-4"/></svg></span>';
        const panel=document.createElement('div');panel.className='ops-notes-panel';panel.id=panelId;panel.hidden=true;
        const noteArea=document.createElement('textarea');noteArea.dataset.manual='note';noteArea.placeholder='רישום הערות טכנאי תפעול';noteArea.setAttribute('aria-label','הערות טכנאי התפעול');
        noteArea.value=record?(manual(record).note||''):'';
        panel.append(noteArea);meta.append(noteButton);
        const elapsed=summary.querySelector('.elapsed-chip');if(elapsed)meta.append(elapsed);
        meta.append(panel);summary.append(meta);
      }
      const improve=card.querySelector('[data-improvement]'),treatment=card.querySelector('[data-pane="treatment"]');
      if(improve&&treatment){improve.classList.add('status-improve');const complete=treatment.querySelector('.r13-complete');if(complete)complete.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',improve);else treatment.prepend(improve);}
      if(!card.closest('.row-detail-dialog')){
        const detailButton=document.createElement('button');detailButton.className='maintenance-open';detailButton.type='button';
        detailButton.dataset.openWork=card.dataset.key;detailButton.innerHTML='<span>פתיחת פרטי ההודעה</span>'+icons.arrow;card.append(detailButton);
      }
      card.querySelectorAll('.manual select,.manual textarea,.manual input').forEach(input=>{
        const label=input.previousElementSibling;
        if(label?.tagName==='LABEL'){input.id=`phone-${card.dataset.key.replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/ig,'-')}-${input.dataset.manual}-${card.closest('.row-detail-dialog')?'detail':'card'}`;label.htmlFor=input.id;}
      });
    });
    root.querySelectorAll('.rows-table button[data-op="night"]').forEach(button=>button.remove());
    root.querySelectorAll('.rows-table button[data-op]').forEach(button=>button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(button.classList.contains('active'))));
  }
  function visibleRecord(recordKey){return Array.from(cards.querySelectorAll('[data-key]')).find(el=>el.dataset.key===recordKey&&!el.closest('.row-detail-overlay'));}
  function snapshot(){
    const focused=document.activeElement, activeRecord=focused?.closest?.('[data-key]'),overlay=cards.querySelector('.row-detail-overlay');
    let anchor=activeRecord||visibleRecord(lastRecordKey);
    if(pageName!=='home')anchor=null;
    else if(!anchor||anchor.getBoundingClientRect().bottom<0||anchor.getBoundingClientRect().top>innerHeight)anchor=Array.from(cards.querySelectorAll('.department-box [data-key]')).find(el=>{const rect=el.getBoundingClientRect();return rect.top<innerHeight&&rect.bottom>0;});
    return {
      panes:resetPanes?[]:Array.from(cards.querySelectorAll('.work-card')).map(card=>({key:card.dataset.key,tab:card.querySelector('[data-tab].active')?.dataset.tab,more:!!card.querySelector('.mobile-more[open]'),notesOpen:!!card.querySelector('[data-toggle-ops-notes][aria-expanded="true"]'),notesDialog:!!card.closest('.row-detail-dialog')})),
      anchorKey:anchor?.dataset.key,anchorY:anchor?.getBoundingClientRect().top,
      focused:activeRecord&&focused?.dataset?.manual?{key:activeRecord.dataset.key,field:focused.dataset.manual,start:focused.selectionStart,end:focused.selectionEnd}:null,
      overlayKey:overlay?.querySelector('[data-key]')?.dataset.key,modalScroll:overlay?.querySelector('.row-detail-dialog')?.scrollTop||0
    };
  }
  function restore(saved){
    if(saved.overlayKey){const r=SAP.find(row=>key(row)===saved.overlayKey);if(r)openRowDetail(r);}
    const cardMap=new Map();cards.querySelectorAll('.work-card').forEach(card=>{const list=cardMap.get(card.dataset.key)||[];list.push(card);cardMap.set(card.dataset.key,list);});
    saved.panes.forEach(savedPane=>(cardMap.get(savedPane.key)||[]).forEach(card=>{
      if(screenMode()==='maintenance'&&!card.closest('.row-detail-dialog'))return;
      if(savedPane.tab){card.querySelectorAll('[data-tab]').forEach(tab=>{const active=tab.dataset.tab===savedPane.tab;tab.classList.toggle('active',active);tab.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(active));});card.querySelectorAll('[data-pane]').forEach(pane=>pane.classList.toggle('hidden',pane.dataset.pane!==savedPane.tab));}
      if(savedPane.more&&card.querySelector('.mobile-more'))card.querySelector('.mobile-more').open=true;
      if(savedPane.notesOpen&&savedPane.notesDialog===!!card.closest('.row-detail-dialog'))setNotesOpen(card,true);
    }));
    if(saved.focused){
      const list=cardMap.get(saved.focused.key)||[], parent=saved.overlayKey?list.find(el=>el.closest('.row-detail-overlay')):list[0];
      const field=parent?.querySelector(`[data-manual="${saved.focused.field}"]`);
      if(field){field.focus({preventScroll:true});if(typeof saved.focused.start==='number'&&typeof field.setSelectionRange==='function'){try{field.setSelectionRange(saved.focused.start,saved.focused.end);}catch{}}}
    }
    if(saved.overlayKey){const dialog=cards.querySelector('.row-detail-dialog');if(dialog)dialog.scrollTop=saved.modalScroll;}
    else if(saved.anchorKey){const anchor=visibleRecord(saved.anchorKey);if(anchor)window.scrollBy({top:anchor.getBoundingClientRect().top-saved.anchorY,behavior:'instant'});}
  }
  const baseDraw=draw;
  draw=function(){
    if(!mq.matches||restoring){baseDraw();return;}
    const saved=snapshot();baseDraw();
    if(prioritySummaryMode&&screenMode()==='operations'){
      cards.innerHTML=prioritySummaryHTML();
      byId('departmentNav').innerHTML='';
      byId('mobileResultCount').textContent='סיכום תעדוף';
    }else enhanceCards();
    syncChrome();syncPrioritySessionBar();
    restoring=true;try{if(!prioritySummaryMode)restore(saved);}finally{restoring=false;resetPanes=false;}
  };
  const baseOpen=openRowDetail,baseClose=closeRowDetail;
  openRowDetail=function(record){
    if(!record)return;
    detailReturnKey=key(record);baseOpen(record);
    if(!mq.matches)return;
    const overlay=cards.querySelector('.row-detail-overlay'), dialog=overlay?.querySelector('.row-detail-dialog');
    if(dialog){const header=document.createElement('div');header.className='mobile-dialog-header';header.append(dialog.querySelector('.row-detail-title'),dialog.querySelector('.row-detail-close'));dialog.prepend(header);header.querySelector('.row-detail-title').textContent=`הודעה ${notificationNo(record)||''}`;}
    enhanceCards(overlay||cards);
  };
  closeRowDetail=function(){baseClose();};
  const baseSave=saveFrom;
  saveFrom=function(el){
    if(mq.matches&&el.dataset.manual==='operational'){
      const id=el.closest('[data-key]')?.dataset.key;
      if(screenMode()==='maintenance'){el.value=currentPriority(id);return;}
      const deadline=Date.now()+PRIORITY_FOLD_DELAY;
      const selected=applyWorkPriority(id,el.value,false);
      if(selected!==null)queuePriorityFold(id,selected,deadline);
      return;
    }
    if(mq.matches&&el.matches('textarea[data-manual],input[data-manual]')){
      const r=SAP.find(row=>key(row)===el.closest('[data-key]')?.dataset.key);if(!r)return;
      Store.set(key(r),{[el.dataset.manual]:el.value});
      if(el.dataset.manual==='note'){
        cards.querySelectorAll('[data-key]').forEach(other=>{
          if(other.dataset.key===key(r))other.querySelectorAll('textarea[data-manual="note"]').forEach(field=>{if(field!==el)field.value=el.value;});
        });
      }
      const recent=Array.from(el.closest('.manual')?.querySelectorAll('label')||[]).find(label=>label.textContent==='פעולות אחרונות');
      if(recent?.nextElementSibling)recent.nextElementSibling.textContent=Store.get(key(r)).updatedAt;
      return;
    }
    baseSave(el);
  };
  function flushActiveInput(){const field=document.activeElement;if(mq.matches&&field?.matches('textarea[data-manual],input[data-manual]')){clearTimeout(field._t);saveFrom(field);}}
  function resetSearchAndFilters(){clearTimeout(searchTimer);searchInput.value='';byId('search').value='';filterIds.forEach(id=>byId(id).value='');byId('workingPriority').value='__urgent__';syncQuickFilterState();update();}
  function runSearch(){clearTimeout(searchTimer);byId('search').value=searchInput.value;update();}

  bottomNav.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    const button=event.target.closest('[data-mobile-page]');if(!button)return;
    flushActiveInput();showPage(button.dataset.mobilePage);
    if(button.dataset.mobilePage==='home')window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'});
  });
  byId('mobileWorkspaceClose').addEventListener('click',()=>showPage('home'));
  mobileScreenButtons().forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    flushActiveInput();const selected=button.dataset.mobileScreen;
    if(selected!==screenMode()){Store.setUi({mobileScreen:selected});resetPanes=true;applyScreenMode();draw();}
    showPage('home');window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'instant'});
  }));
  byId('mobileDeptList').addEventListener('click',event=>{
    const choice=event.target.closest('[data-department-choice]');if(!choice)return;
    let code=choice.dataset.departmentChoice;
    const field=byId('workingDepartment');
    if(code&&!Array.from(field.options).some(option=>option.value===code)){
      // Existing filters have no unassigned option; showing all and scrolling keeps source logic intact.
      field.value='';syncQuickFilterState();update();showPage('home');document.getElementById('dept-'+code)?.scrollIntoView({block:'start'});return;
    }
    field.value=code;syncQuickFilterState();update();showPage('home');window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'instant'});
  });
  workspace.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    const open=event.target.closest('[data-open-work]');
    if(open){const r=SAP.find(row=>key(row)===open.dataset.openWork);if(r)openRowDetail(r);return;}
    const action=event.target.closest('[data-mobile-tool]')?.dataset.mobileTool;if(!action)return;
    if(action.startsWith('upload-')){byId(action.slice(7)+'File').click();return;}
    if(action==='report'){showPage('home');openMergedReport();setReportTab('table');}
    if(action==='card-layout'){showPage('home');openMergedReport();setReportTab('layout');}
    if(action==='table-layout'){showPage('home');openMergedReport();setReportTab('tableLayout');}
    if(action==='system'){showPage('home');byId('openScreenDesignerFixed').click();}
  });
  ['notifications','orders','permits','preventive'].forEach(role=>{const f=byId(role+'File');if(f)f.addEventListener('change',()=>{if(pageName==='tools')setTimeout(refreshTools,300);});});
  searchInput.addEventListener('input',()=>{byId('mobileClearSearch').hidden=!searchInput.value;clearTimeout(searchTimer);searchTimer=setTimeout(runSearch,180);});
  searchInput.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Enter'){event.preventDefault();runSearch();searchInput.blur();}});
  byId('mobileClearSearch').addEventListener('click',()=>{searchInput.value='';runSearch();searchInput.focus();});
  byId('mobileSearchReset').addEventListener('click',resetSearchAndFilters);
  byId('mobileSearchShowHome').addEventListener('click',()=>{runSearch();searchInput.blur();showPage('home');window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'instant'});});
  filterButton.addEventListener('click',()=>setFilters(!filters.classList.contains('mobile-filters-open')));
  byId('rowViewTop').addEventListener('click',()=>setFilters(false));
  byId('workingApplyFilters').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(mq.matches)setFilters(false);});
  const baseClear=byId('clearQuickFilters').onclick;
  byId('clearQuickFilters').onclick=event=>{if(mq.matches){clearTimeout(searchTimer);searchInput.value='';byId('search').value='';}baseClear(event);if(mq.matches){byId('workingPriority').value='__urgent__';syncQuickFilterState();update();}syncChrome();};
  byId('mobileCloseTools').addEventListener('click',closeTools);
  byId('mobileBackdrop').addEventListener('click',closeTools);
  byId('mobileEditSystem').addEventListener('click',()=>{closeTools();byId('openScreenDesignerFixed').click();});
  cards.addEventListener('pointerdown',event=>{
    const record=event.target.closest('.work-card[data-key],tr[data-key]');
    if(record)lastRecordKey=record.dataset.key;
    const button=event.target.closest('[data-op]');
    pressedWorkPriority=button&&record?{id:record.dataset.key,choice:button.dataset.op}:null;
  },true);
  cards.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{pressedWorkPriority=null;},true);
  cards.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    if(!mq.matches)return;
    const record=event.target.closest('[data-key]');
    const expand=event.target.closest('[data-expand-priority]');
    if(expand){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();expandPriorityCard(expand.dataset.expandPriority);return;}
    const noteToggle=event.target.closest('[data-toggle-ops-notes]');
    if(noteToggle&&record){setNotesOpen(record,noteToggle.getAttribute('aria-expanded')!=='true');event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();return;}
    const open=event.target.closest('[data-open-work]');
    if(open){const r=SAP.find(row=>key(row)===open.dataset.openWork);if(r)openRowDetail(r);event.stopPropagation();return;}
    if(false&&screenMode()==='maintenance'&&record&&!record.closest('.row-detail-overlay')&&record.classList.contains('work-card')&&!event.target.closest('button,input,select,textarea,label,a,summary')){
      const r=SAP.find(row=>key(row)===record.dataset.key);if(r)openRowDetail(r);event.stopPropagation();return;
    }
    const op=event.target.closest('[data-op]');
    if(op&&screenMode()==='maintenance'){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();return;}
    if(op&&record){
      // This capture handler owns the tap; the old bubbling handler must not run too.
      event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();
      const k=record.dataset.key,choice=op.dataset.op,pressed=pressedWorkPriority;
      pressedWorkPriority=null;
      if(pressed&&event.detail!==0&&(pressed.id!==k||pressed.choice!==choice))return;
      const deadline=Date.now()+PRIORITY_FOLD_DELAY;
      flushActiveInput();
      const selected=applyWorkPriority(k,choice,true);
      if(selected!==null){
        queuePriorityFold(k,selected,deadline);
        if(!volatileStorage)message(selected?'התעדוף עודכן: '+operationalText(selected):'בחירת התעדוף בוטלה');
      }
      return;
    }
    if(event.target.closest('[data-mobile-reset]'))resetSearchAndFilters();
    if(event.target.closest('[data-tab]'))requestAnimationFrame(()=>enhanceCards());
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
    if(!mq.matches)return;
    if(event.key==='Escape'&&!document.body.classList.contains('row-detail-open')){showPage('home');setFilters(false);}
    const dialog=cards.querySelector('.row-detail-dialog');
    if(dialog&&event.key==='Tab'){
      const items=Array.from(dialog.querySelectorAll('button,input,select,textarea,summary,[tabindex="0"]')).filter(el=>el.getClientRects().length&&!el.disabled),first=items[0],last=items.at(-1);
      if(event.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===first){event.preventDefault();last?.focus();}
      else if(!event.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){event.preventDefault();first?.focus();}
    }
  });
  window.addEventListener('pagehide',flushActiveInput);
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)flushActiveInput();});
  function resizeMode(){
    if(mq.matches===wasMobile)return;wasMobile=mq.matches;
    workspace.hidden=true;pageName='home';document.body.classList.remove('mobile-panel-open');setFilters(false);closeTools();applyScreenMode();
    state.viewMode=(mq.matches?Store.ui().mobileViewMode:Store.ui().viewMode)==='table'?'table':'cards';
    tickerTitle.textContent=originalTickerText;permitTickerSignature=null;resetPanes=true;draw();
    if(mq.matches)showPage('home');
  }
  mq.addEventListener('change',resizeMode);
  if(mq.matches){state.viewMode=Store.ui().mobileViewMode==='table'?'table':'cards';applyScreenMode();baseDraw();enhanceCards();syncChrome();showPage('home');}
  window.TOVATI_MOBILE={version:'1.4.2',patchPriority,enhance:enhanceCards,setNotesOpen,breakpoint:1024,refresh:()=>{enhanceCards();syncChrome();},showPage};
})();

(() => {
  'use strict';
  const nav=document.getElementById('tovatiTopNav');
  const panel=document.getElementById('tovatiTopPanel');
  const title=document.getElementById('tovatiTopPanelTitle');
  const content=document.getElementById('tovatiTopPanelContent');
  const closeButton=document.getElementById('tovatiTopPanelClose');
  const displayStateKey='tovati.display.closed';
  let returnFocus=null, localExited=false;
  const e=value=>String(value??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));
  const num=n=>Number(n).toLocaleString('he-IL');
  const records=()=>typeof SAP!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(SAP)?SAP:[];
  function flushInput(){
    const field=document.activeElement;
    if(field?.matches('textarea[data-manual],input[data-manual]')&&typeof saveFrom==='function'){
      clearTimeout(field._t);saveFrom(field);
    }
  }
  function activeAction(action=''){
    nav.querySelectorAll('[aria-expanded]').forEach(button=>button.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(button.dataset.topAction===action)));
  }
  function openPanel(action,label,html){
    returnFocus=document.activeElement;
    title.textContent=label;content.innerHTML=html;content.scrollTop=0;
    closeButton.hidden=false;activeAction(action);
    document.body.classList.add('tovati-top-panel-open');
    if(!panel.open){if(typeof panel.showModal==='function')panel.showModal();else panel.setAttribute('open','');}
    title.focus({preventScroll:true});
  }
  function closePanel(restoreFocus=true){
    if(localExited)return;
    if(typeof panel.close==='function')panel.close();else panel.removeAttribute('open');
    document.body.classList.remove('tovati-top-panel-open');activeAction();
    if(restoreFocus&&returnFocus?.isConnected)returnFocus.focus({preventScroll:true});
  }
  function recordButton(row,extra=''){
    return `<button class="top-panel-item" type="button" data-top-work="${e(key(row))}"><span class="top-item-meta">הודעה <bdi>${e(notificationNo(row)||'ללא מספר')}</bdi> · ${e(maintenanceDepartmentName(row)||'')}</span><span class="top-item-title">${e(description(row)||'ללא תיאור')}</span>${extra}</button>`;
  }
  function showAlerts(){
    const urgent=records().filter(row=>['דחוף','חירום'].includes(String(priorityValue(row)).trim())&&!isClosed(row)&&!manual(row).operational);
    const requests=typeof DATASETS==='undefined'?[]:DATASETS.permits.filter(p=>String(val(p,ALIASES.permitStatus)||'').trim()==='בקשה');
    const permitItems=requests.map(p=>{
      const permit=val(p,ALIASES.permit)||'',order=val(p,ALIASES.order)||'';
      const desc=val(p,['תיאור היתר','תאור היתר',...ALIASES.description])||'ללא תיאור';
      return `<article class="top-panel-item"><span class="top-item-meta">סטטוס: בקשה · היתר <bdi>${e(permit)}</bdi>${order?` · הזמנה <bdi>${e(order)}</bdi>`:''}</span><span class="top-item-title">${e(desc)}</span></article>`;
    }).join('');
    openPanel('alerts','התראות',`<p class="top-panel-intro">לפי הדוחות והעדכונים במכשיר הזה, ללא התראות שרת.</p><section class="top-panel-section"><h3>דחופות שטרם תועדפו <b>${num(urgent.length)}</b></h3><div class="top-panel-list">${urgent.length?urgent.map(row=>recordButton(row)).join(''):'<div class="top-panel-empty">אין עבודות דחופות פתוחות שממתינות לתעדוף.</div>'}</div></section><section class="top-panel-section"><h3>היתרים בסטטוס בקשה <b>${num(requests.length)}</b></h3><div class="top-panel-list">${permitItems||'<div class="top-panel-empty">אין היתרים בסטטוס בקשה בדוח.</div>'}</div></section>`);
  }
  function showMessages(){
    const notes=records().map(row=>({row,values:manual(row)})).filter(item=>String(item.values.note||'').trim()).sort((a,b)=>String(b.values.updatedAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.values.updatedAt||'')));
    openPanel('messages','הודעות',`<p class="top-panel-intro">הערות טכנאי התפעול שנשמרו בכרטיסיות במכשיר הזה. שליחת הודעות בין משתמשים אינה מחוברת בקובץ המקומי.</p><div class="top-panel-list">${notes.length?notes.map(item=>recordButton(item.row,`<p class="top-item-note">${e(item.values.note)}</p>`)).join(''):'<div class="top-panel-empty">אין עדיין הערות שמורות.<br>הערות שתוסיף בכרטיסיות יופיעו כאן.</div>'}</div>`);
  }
  function showFeed(){
    closePanel(false);
    window.TOVATI_MOBILE?.showPage('home');
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>document.getElementById('cards')?.scrollIntoView({block:'start',behavior:'smooth'}));
  }
  function confirmExit(){
    openPanel('logout','התנתקות','<p class="top-panel-intro">לסגור את התצוגה במכשיר הזה? הנתונים והתעדופים לא יימחקו.</p><div class="top-exit-info">הקובץ פועל מקומית ללא חשבון מחובר לשרת. זוהי יציאה מהתצוגה בלבד, ולא נעילת אבטחה.</div><div class="top-panel-actions"><button type="button" data-exit-cancel>ביטול</button><button class="top-primary" type="button" data-exit-confirm>יציאה מהתצוגה</button></div>');
  }
  function setDisplayExited(exited){
    localExited=exited;document.body.classList.toggle('tovati-local-exited',exited);
    for(const selector of ['.app','#tovatiTopNav','#mobileBottomNav','#mobileWorkspace']){
      const target=document.querySelector(selector);if(target)target.inert=exited;
    }
    try{if(exited)sessionStorage.setItem(displayStateKey,'1');else sessionStorage.removeItem(displayStateKey);}catch{/* Local viewers can disallow session storage. */}
  }
  function showExited(){
    window.TOVATI_MOBILE?.showPage('home');
    setDisplayExited(true);
    openPanel('logout','התצוגה סגורה','<p class="top-panel-intro">הנתונים לא נמחקו. אפשר לחזור לתצוגה ולהמשיך לעבוד.</p><div class="top-exit-info">מצב מקומי בלבד — אין בקובץ אימות משתמש או נעילת אבטחה.</div><div class="top-panel-actions"><button class="top-primary" type="button" data-display-return>חזרה למערכת</button></div>');
    closeButton.hidden=true;
    content.querySelector('[data-display-return]').focus({preventScroll:true});
  }
  // Flush a typed note before the menu button takes focus; do not clear any work data.
  nav.addEventListener('pointerdown',flushInput,true);
  nav.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    const action=event.target.closest('[data-top-action]')?.dataset.topAction;
    if(!action)return;flushInput();
    if(action==='alerts')showAlerts();
    if(action==='messages')showMessages();
    if(action==='feed')showFeed();
    if(action==='logout')confirmExit();
  });
  closeButton.addEventListener('click',()=>closePanel());
  panel.addEventListener('cancel',event=>{event.preventDefault();if(!localExited)closePanel();});
  panel.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    if(event.target!==panel||localExited)return;
    const rect=panel.getBoundingClientRect();
    if(event.clientX<rect.left||event.clientX>rect.right||event.clientY<rect.top||event.clientY>rect.bottom)closePanel();
  });
  content.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    const work=event.target.closest('[data-top-work]');
    if(work){const row=records().find(r=>key(r)===work.dataset.topWork);if(row){closePanel(false);window.TOVATI_MOBILE?.showPage('home');openRowDetail(row);}return;}
    if(event.target.closest('[data-exit-cancel]'))closePanel();
    if(event.target.closest('[data-exit-confirm]'))showExited();
    if(event.target.closest('[data-display-return]')){setDisplayExited(false);closePanel(false);nav.querySelector('[data-am-home]')?.focus({preventScroll:true});window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'instant'});}
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
    if(!panel.open)return;
    // Keep the existing page/card keyboard handlers from acting behind this dialog.
    if(event.key==='Escape'){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();if(!localExited)closePanel();}
    if(event.key==='Tab'){
      event.stopImmediatePropagation();
      const items=Array.from(panel.querySelectorAll('button,[href],input,textarea,select,[tabindex="0"]')).filter(el=>!el.disabled&&el.getClientRects().length);
      const first=items[0],last=items[items.length-1];
      if(event.shiftKey&&(document.activeElement===first||document.activeElement===title)){event.preventDefault();last?.focus();}
      else if(!event.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){event.preventDefault();first?.focus();}
    }
  },true);
  try{if(sessionStorage.getItem(displayStateKey)==='1')showExited();}catch{}
})();

;

function compactStateForStorage(state){

function hydrateStoredState(raw,embeddedState){
 const parsed=JSON.parse(raw);
 if(parsed?.format===TOVATI_COMPACT_STORAGE&&parsed.state){
  const base=embeddedState?clone(embeddedState):fresh(),saved=parsed.state;

  return validateState(merged);
 }
 return validateState(parsed);
}

const beforeLocalSources=clone(DATASETS);
function validateState(x){
 if(!x||x.schema!==1||typeof x!=='object'||Array.isArray(x))throw Error('קובץ הפיילוט אינו במבנה נתמך.');
 if(x.pmOrders===undefined)x={...x,pmOrders:[]};
 const out=fresh();
 autoValidateStored(x);
 for(const k of ['automations','automationInbox','automationRuns'])out[k]=cleanObject(x[k]||[]);
 out.automationSeen=cleanObject(x.automationSeen||{});
 for(const k of ['workers','assignments','plans','pmOrders','tasks','delays','timeEntries','posts','chatMessages','audit']){
  if(!Array.isArray(x[k])||x[k].length>MAXROWS)throw Error('רשימה חסרה או גדולה מדי: '+k);
  out[k]=cleanObject(x[k]);
  const ids=new Set();for(const r of out[k]){if(!r||typeof r!=='object'||typeof r.id!=='string'||!/^[a-zA-Z0-9:_-]{1,200}$/.test(r.id)||ids.has(r.id))throw Error('מזהה חסר או כפול ברשימה '+k);ids.add(r.id);}
 }
 for(const k of ['closures','reviews','seen','chatRead','importMeta','profile']){if(x[k]&&typeof x[k]==='object'&&!Array.isArray(x[k]))out[k]=cleanObject(x[k]);}
 out.userGroups=validateUserGroups(cleanObject(x.userGroups||[]));
 out.workers.forEach(w=>{if(w.userSchema===1)validateUserMetadata(w);});
 out.revision=Number.isSafeInteger(x.revision)?x.revision:0;
 if(x.sources){out.sources={};for(const k of Object.keys(roles)){out.sources[k]=validateRows(k,x.sources[k]);}}
 for(const a of out.assignments){if(!validDate(a.date)||!Number.isFinite(timeMin(a.start))||!(timeMin(a.end)>timeMin(a.start))||!Array.isArray(a.workerIds)||typeof a.workRef!=='string')throw Error('תזמון לא תקין בגיבוי.');}
 for(const w of out.workers){if(typeof w.name!=='string'||!w.name||typeof w.code!=='string'||!Array.isArray(w.days)||w.days.some(d=>!Number.isInteger(d)||d<0||d>6)||!Array.isArray(w.leave)||w.leave.some(d=>!validDate(d))||!(w.capacity>0&&w.capacity<=24)||!(timeMin(w.end)>timeMin(w.start)))throw Error('רשומת עובד לא תקינה בגיבוי.');}
 if(new Set(out.workers.map(w=>w.code)).size!==out.workers.length)throw Error('מספרי עובד כפולים בגיבוי.');
 for(const a of out.assignments){if(!Object.hasOwn(statusNames,a.status)||a.workerIds.some(id=>!out.workers.some(w=>w.id===id))||!a.snapshot||typeof a.snapshot.title!=='string')throw Error('שיוך עובד או מצב שיבוץ לא תקינים בגיבוי.');}
 for(const p of out.plans){if(typeof p.title!=='string'||typeof p.asset!=='string'||!validDate(p.nextDue)||!Number.isInteger(p.intervalDays)||p.intervalDays<1||p.intervalDays>3650||!(p.duration>0&&p.duration<=16))throw Error('תוכנית מונעת לא תקינה בגיבוי.');}
 for(const p of out.pmOrders){if(typeof p.title!=='string'||typeof p.order!=='string'||typeof p.dept!=='string'||(p.due&&!validDate(p.due))||!(p.duration>0&&p.duration<=24)||(p.workHours!==undefined&&!(Number(p.workHours)>=0)))throw Error('פקודת תחזוקה מונעת לא תקינה בגיבוי.');}
 for(const t of out.tasks){if(typeof t.ref!=='string'||!validDate(t.due)||typeof t.title!=='string')throw Error('משימה מקומית לא תקינה בגיבוי.');}
 for(const d of out.delays){if(!validDate(d.due)||!['open','resolved'].includes(d.state)||typeof d.workRef!=='string'||typeof d.text!=='string')throw Error('עיכוב לא תקין בגיבוי.');}
 for(const p of out.posts){if(typeof p.text!=='string'||typeof p.author!=='string'||!Array.isArray(p.comments)||p.comments.some(c=>typeof c.text!=='string'||typeof c.author!=='string'))throw Error('הודעה לא תקינה בגיבוי.');}
 for(const t of out.timeEntries){if(!validDate(t.date)||!(t.hours>0&&t.hours<=24))throw Error('דיווח שעות לא תקין בגיבוי.');}
 r10ValidateStateExtras(x,out);r11ValidateDrafts(x,out);r12ValidatePublications(x,out);r15ValidateState(x,out);r17ValidateChats(x,out);
 return out;
}
function cleanObject(x,depth=0){if(depth>30)throw Error('מבנה נתונים עמוק מדי.');if(Array.isArray(x))return x.map(v=>cleanObject(v,depth+1));if(x&&typeof x==='object'){const o={};for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(x)){if(['__proto__','constructor','prototype'].includes(k))throw Error('שדה לא בטוח בנתונים.');o[k]=cleanObject(v,depth+1);}return o;}if(typeof x==='string'&&x.length>500000&&!(x.length<=140000000&&/^data:(?:image|video)\/[a-zA-Z0-9.+-]+;base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/]+=*$/.test(x)))throw Error('שדה ארוך מדי.');return x;}
function persistenceUI(){document.body.classList.remove('pilot-volatile');const p=$p('pilotPersistence');if(p){p.textContent='';p.hidden=true;}const w=$p('pilotStorageWarning');if(w)w.hidden=true;}
function auditDraft(d,action,ref='',detail=''){d.audit.unshift({id:uid('audit'),at:now(),actor:d.profile.name||'מפעיל מקומי',action,ref,detail});if(d.audit.length>5000)d.audit.length=5000;}
// R9: canonical per-card data remains in Store. Only priority audit events use
// this compact journal; no large reports, media, users, or schedules are rewritten.
const PRIORITY_AUDIT_KEY=DBKEY+':priority-audit-v1';
function readPriorityAudit(){
 try{const rows=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PRIORITY_AUDIT_KEY)||'[]');return Array.isArray(rows)?rows.filter(x=>x&&typeof x.id==='string'&&x.id.startsWith('priorityaudit-')&&typeof x.at==='string'&&typeof x.ref==='string'&&typeof x.actor==='string'&&typeof x.action==='string'&&typeof x.detail==='string').slice(0,500):[];}catch{return [];}
}
function mergePriorityAudit(target=db,rows=readPriorityAudit()){
 const existing=new Set((target.audit||[]).map(x=>x.id)),missing=rows.filter(x=>!existing.has(x.id));
 if(missing.length)target.audit=[...missing,...(target.audit||[])].sort((a,b)=>String(b.at).localeCompare(String(a.at))).slice(0,5000);
 return target;
}
function recordPriorityAudit(ref,selected){
 const event={id:uid('priorityaudit'),at:now(),actor:db.profile.name||'מפעיל מקומי',action:'עדכון תעדוף',ref,detail:opNames[selected]||'ללא'};
 const journal=[event,...readPriorityAudit()].slice(0,500);
 try{localStorage.setItem(PRIORITY_AUDIT_KEY,JSON.stringify(journal));}catch(e){}
 mergePriorityAudit(db,journal);
 window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('tovati:committed',{detail:{action:event.action,ref,priorityOnly:true}}));
}
function commit(action,ref,mutate,detail=''){
 mergePriorityAudit();
 if(!volatile){const stored=localStorage.getItem(DBKEY);if(stored&&storedStateRevision(stored)!==db.revision)throw Error('הנתונים השתנו בלשונית אחרת. סגור את הטופס ורענן את המסך לפני שמירה.');}
 const d=clone(db);mutate(d);auditDraft(d,action,ref,detail);d.revision++;d.updatedAt=now();
 try{persistDb(d);}catch(e){volatile=true;readError=e.message;}
 db=d;persistenceUI();refreshMainScheduling();if(document.getElementById('tovatiTestIdentity'))refreshTestIdentity();window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('tovati:committed',{detail:{action,ref}}));return d;
}
function saved(text='העדכון נשמר'){toast(text);}
function toast(text){const t=$p('pilotToast');t.textContent=text;t.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(toastTimer);toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),3600);}
function requireText(s,label,max=1500){s=String(s??'').trim();if(!s)throw Error('יש למלא '+label+'.');if(s.length>max)throw Error(label+' ארוך מדי.');return s;}
function buildMainRecords(){return SAP.map(r=>({ref:key(r),raw:r,title:description(r)||'ללא תיאור',notification:String(notificationNo(r)||''),order:String(orderNo(r)||''),dept:maintenanceDepartment(r)||'',type:val(notificationRow(r),['סוג','סוג הודעה'])||'',priority:priorityValue(r)||'',permit:permitNo(r)||'',permitStatus:permitStatusValue(r)||'',source:'הודעה',created:notificationDate(r)}));}
let sourceCache=null,sourceCacheLength=-1,mainCache=[],orderDeptCache=new Map(),recordCache=null,recordCacheOrders=null,recordCacheSource=null,recordCacheRevision=-1,recordMap=new Map();
function mainRecords(){if(sourceCache!==SAP||sourceCacheLength!==SAP.length){sourceCache=SAP;sourceCacheLength=SAP.length;mainCache=buildMainRecords();orderDeptCache=new Map(mainCache.map(r=>[normId(r.order),r.dept]));}return mainCache;}
function records(){mainRecords();if(recordCacheSource!==SAP||recordCacheOrders!==DATASETS.orders||recordCacheRevision!==db.revision){recordCacheSource=SAP;recordCacheOrders=DATASETS.orders;recordCacheRevision=db.revision;recordCache=buildRecords();recordMap=new Map(recordCache.map(r=>[r.ref,r]));}return recordCache;}
function buildRecords(){
 const list=mainRecords().slice(),orders=new Set(list.map(r=>normId(r.order)).filter(Boolean));
 DATASETS.orders.forEach((o,i)=>{const id=normId(val(o,ALIASES.order));if(!id||orders.has(id))return;orders.add(id);const ps=DATASETS.permits.filter(p=>normId(val(p,ALIASES.order))===id);list.push({ref:'unlinked-order:'+id,title:String(val(o,ALIASES.description)||'ללא תיאור'),notification:String(val(o,ALIASES.notification)||''),order:id,dept:String(val(o,['קבוצת מתכנן','מחלקה'])||deptForCenter(val(o,ALIASES.center))||''),type:String(val(o,['סוג'])||''),priority:String(val(o,ALIASES.priority)||''),permit:ps.map(p=>val(p,ALIASES.permit)).join(', '),permitStatus:ps.map(p=>val(p,ALIASES.permitStatus)).join(', '),source:'הזמנה',created:String(val(o,ALIASES.created)||'').slice(0,10),orderRaw:o});});
 db.tasks.forEach(t=>list.push({...t,source:'תוכנית מונעת',priority:t.priority||'רגיל',notification:'',order:'',permit:'',permitStatus:''}));
 pmOrdersSource().forEach(p=>list.push({ref:'pmorder:'+p.id,title:p.title,notification:'',order:p.order||'',dept:p.dept||deptForCenter(p.section),section:p.section||'',type:'PM',priority:p.priority||'רגיל',permit:'',permitStatus:'',source:'פקודת תחזוקה מונעת',created:p.due||'',due:p.due||'',asset:p.asset||'',duration:p.duration||1,workHours:p.workHours||p.duration||1,plan:p.plan||'',status:p.status||'',pmOrderId:p.id,pmRaw:p.raw||null}));return list;
}
const record=ref=>{records();return recordMap.get(ref)||null;};
const refState=ref=>{const r=workCardForKey(ref);return r?manual(r):{};};
const activeAssignments=()=>db.assignments.filter(a=>a.status!=='cancelled');
const workerName=id=>db.workers.find(w=>w.id===id)?.name||'עובד שלא נמצא';
const workTitle=ref=>record(ref)?.title||db.assignments.find(a=>a.workRef===ref)?.snapshot?.title||'עבודה שאינה בדוח הנוכחי';
const sourceDate=()=>Object.values(DATASETS).flatMap(rows=>rows.map(r=>String(val(r,ALIASES.created)||'').slice(0,10))).filter(validDate).sort().at(-1)||'';
function permitWarning(r){return `תכנון בלבד. ${r?.permit?'היתר בדוח: '+r.permit+' · '+(r.permitStatus||'ללא סטטוס'):'לא נמצא היתר מקושר בדוח'}. לפני ביצוע נדרש אימות היתר, בידודים ותנאי עבודה במערכת הארגונית.`;}
function snapshot(r){return {title:r.title,order:r.order,notification:r.notification,dept:r.dept,source:r.source,permit:r.permit,permitStatus:r.permitStatus};}
function workerData(input,id){
 const name=requireText(input.name,'שם עובד',80),code=requireText(input.code,'מספר עובד',40);
 if(db.workers.some(w=>w.id!==id&&w.code===code))throw Error('מספר העובד כבר קיים.');
 const capacity=Number(input.capacity),start=input.start||'06:00',end=input.end||'18:00',days=(input.days||[]).map(Number);
 if(!(capacity>0&&capacity<=24))throw Error('קיבולת יומית צריכה להיות בין 0 ל־24 שעות.');
 if(!(timeMin(end)>timeMin(start)))throw Error('שעות זמינות לא תקינות.');
 if(!days.length||days.some(d=>!Number.isInteger(d)||d<0||d>6))throw Error('בחר ימי זמינות.');
 const leave=String(input.leave||'').split(/[\s,;]+/).filter(Boolean);if(leave.some(d=>!validDate(d)))throw Error('תאריכי היעדרות יש להזין במבנה YYYY-MM-DD.');
 return {id:id||uid('worker'),name,code,dept:String(input.dept||''),skill:String(input.skill||'').trim().slice(0,120),capacity,start,end,days:[...new Set(days)],leave:[...new Set(leave)],active:input.active!==false};
}
function saveWorker(input,id=''){
 const prior=db.workers.find(w=>w.id===id);if(input.branch||prior?.userSchema===1)return saveUser({...normalizedUser(prior),...Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(input).filter(([,v])=>v!==undefined))},id);
 const w=workerData(input,id);commit(id?'עדכון עובד':'הוספת עובד',w.id,d=>{const i=d.workers.findIndex(x=>x.id===w.id);if(i>=0)d.workers[i]={...d.workers[i],...w};else d.workers.push(w);});return w;
}
function schedulingErrors(input,skipId=''){
 const errors=[],r=record(input.workRef);if(currentTestUser()?.branch==='operations')errors.push('תזמון ושיבוץ בבדיקת תפקיד זמינים למשתמש אחזקה.');if(r&&!sessionAllows(r))errors.push('העבודה אינה בהיקף הצפייה של המשתמש שנבחר.');if(!r)errors.push('העבודה לא נמצאת בנתונים הנוכחיים.');
 if(!validDate(input.date))errors.push('יש לבחור תאריך תקין.');
 if(r?.source==='פקודת תחזוקה מונעת'&&validDate(input.date)){const b=pmMonthBounds(r.due);if(b&&(input.date<b.start||input.date>b.end))errors.push('פקודת התחזוקה המונעת ניתנת לשיבוץ בכל יום בתוך '+pmMonthLabel(r.due)+' בלבד.');}
 const start=timeMin(input.start),end=timeMin(input.end);if(!(end>start))errors.push('שעת הסיום חייבת להיות אחרי שעת ההתחלה באותו יום.');
 const ids=[...new Set(input.workerIds||[])];if(!ids.length)errors.push('יש לבחור לפחות עובד אחד לביצוע.');
 const hours=(end-start)/60;
 if(r?.raw&&isClosed(r.raw))errors.push('ההזמנה סגורה בדוח. אין לתזמן אותה מחדש בפיילוט.');
 if(db.closures[input.workRef]?.completed||(r?.raw&&r13StateOf(r.raw).done))errors.push('העבודה הושלמה מקומית. יש לפתוח אותה מחדש במסך הסגירה.');
 ids.forEach(id=>{
  const w=db.workers.find(w=>w.id===id);if(!w||!w.active){errors.push('אחד העובדים חסר או אינו פעיל.');return;}
  if(validDate(input.date)&&(!w.days.includes(new Date(input.date+'T12:00:00').getDay())||w.leave.includes(input.date)))errors.push(w.name+': לא זמין בתאריך הנבחר.');
  if(start<timeMin(w.start)||end>timeMin(w.end))errors.push(w.name+': התזמון מחוץ לשעות הזמינות ('+w.start+'–'+w.end+').');
  const other=activeAssignments().filter(a=>a.id!==skipId&&a.date===input.date&&a.workerIds.includes(id));
  if(other.some(a=>start<timeMin(a.end)&&end>timeMin(a.start)))errors.push(w.name+': קיימת חפיפה עם עבודה אחרת.');
  if(other.reduce((s,a)=>s+(timeMin(a.end)-timeMin(a.start))/60,0)+hours>w.capacity+1e-6)errors.push(w.name+': חריגה מקיבולת '+w.capacity+' שעות ליום.');
 });
 if(activeAssignments().some(a=>a.id!==skipId&&a.workRef===input.workRef&&a.date===input.date&&start<timeMin(a.end)&&end>timeMin(a.start)))errors.push('העבודה כבר משובצת בטווח הזמן הזה.');
 return [...new Set(errors)];
}
function saveAssignment(input,id=''){
 if(mode()!=='maintenance')throw Error('תזמון ושיבוץ מבוצעים במצב אחזקה.');
 const errs=schedulingErrors(input,id);if(errs.length)throw Error(errs.join('\n'));
 const r=record(input.workRef),previous=db.assignments.find(a=>a.id===id);
 const a={id:id||uid('assignment'),workRef:r.ref,snapshot:snapshot(r),date:input.date,start:input.start,end:input.end,workerIds:[...new Set(input.workerIds)],note:String(input.note||'').slice(0,2000),status:previous?.status==='inprogress'?'inprogress':'planned',createdAt:previous?.createdAt||now(),updatedAt:now(),actor:db.profile.name||'מפעיל מקומי'};
 commit(previous?'עדכון שיבוץ':'תזמון עבודה',a.workRef,d=>{const i=d.assignments.findIndex(x=>x.id===a.id);if(i>=0)d.assignments[i]=a;else d.assignments.push(a);},dateLabel(a.date)+' '+a.start+'–'+a.end);return a;
}
function cancelAssignment(id,reason){reason=requireText(reason,'סיבת ביטול');const a=db.assignments.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!a)throw Error('השיבוץ לא נמצא.');if(a.status==='done')throw Error('שיבוץ שהושלם אינו ניתן לביטול; אפשר לתזמן משימה חדשה לאחר פתיחת העבודה למעקב.');commit('ביטול שיבוץ',a.workRef,d=>{const x=d.assignments.find(x=>x.id===id);x.status='cancelled';x.cancelReason=reason;x.updatedAt=now();});}
function setAssignmentStatus(id,status,confirmed=false){const a=db.assignments.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!a)throw Error('השיבוץ לא נמצא.');if(mode()!=='maintenance')throw Error('עדכון ביצוע זמין במצב אחזקה.');if(!['inprogress','planned'].includes(status))throw Error('סטטוס לא נתמך.');if(status==='inprogress'&&!confirmed)throw Error('נדרש לאשר שבוצעו האימותים הארגוניים לפני תחילת ביצוע.');commit('עדכון ביצוע',a.workRef,d=>{const x=d.assignments.find(x=>x.id===id);x.status=status;x.updatedAt=now();});}
function saveDelay(input,id=''){const r=record(input.workRef);if(!r)throw Error('יש לבחור עבודה.');const type=requireText(input.type,'סוג עיכוב'),owner=requireText(input.owner,'אחראי למעקב',80),text=requireText(input.text,'פרטי העיכוב');if(!validDate(input.due))throw Error('תאריך מעקב אינו תקין.');const item={id:id||uid('delay'),workRef:r.ref,type,owner,text,due:input.due,state:'open',createdAt:now()};commit('רישום עיכוב',r.ref,d=>d.delays.push(item));return item;}
function resolveDelay(id,text){text=requireText(text,'פתרון העיכוב');const x=db.delays.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!x)throw Error('עיכוב לא נמצא.');commit('סגירת עיכוב',x.workRef,d=>{const y=d.delays.find(y=>y.id===id);y.state='resolved';y.resolution=text;y.resolvedAt=now();});}
function saveTime(input,id=''){
 const r=record(input.workRef);if(!r)throw Error('יש לבחור עבודה.');const w=db.workers.find(w=>w.id===input.workerId);if(!w)throw Error('יש לבחור עובד.');if(!validDate(input.date)||input.date>today())throw Error('תאריך דיווח חייב להיות תקין ולא בעתיד.');const hours=Number(input.hours);if(!(hours>0&&hours<=24))throw Error('מספר שעות חייב להיות גדול מאפס ועד 24.');
 const used=db.timeEntries.filter(t=>t.id!==id&&!t.voided&&t.workerId===w.id&&t.date===input.date).reduce((s,t)=>s+t.hours,0);if(used+hours>24)throw Error('סך דיווחי העובד ביום הזה עולה על 24 שעות.');
 const text=requireText(input.text,'תיאור ביצוע');const x={id:id||uid('time'),workRef:r.ref,workerId:w.id,date:input.date,hours,text,createdAt:now(),state:'draft',snapshot:snapshot(r)};
 commit('דיווח שעות מקומי',r.ref,d=>{const i=d.timeEntries.findIndex(t=>t.id===x.id);if(i>=0){if(d.timeEntries[i].state!=='draft')throw Error('דיווח שסומן כמוכן אינו ניתן לעריכה. יש לבטל וליצור תיקון.');d.timeEntries[i]=x;}else d.timeEntries.push(x);});return x;
}
function completeWork(input){
 const r=record(input.workRef);if(!r)throw Error('העבודה לא נמצאה.');if(mode()!=='maintenance')throw Error('סגירת ביצוע זמינה במצב אחזקה.');
 if(db.closures[r.ref]?.completed)throw Error('העבודה כבר הושלמה מקומית. אפשר לפתוח אותה מחדש במסך הסגירה.');
 if(!input.done||!input.permitChecked)throw Error('יש לאשר השלמת עבודה ובדיקת הטיפול בהיתר.');
 const text=requireText(input.text,'סיכום ביצוע');if(!sessionAllows(r))throw Error('העבודה אינה בהיקף הצפייה שלך.');if(r13WordCount(text)<4)throw Error('יש לכתוב לפחות 4 מילים על התקלה ודרך הטיפול.');
 commit('השלמה מקומית — ללא עדכון SAP',r.ref,d=>{d.closures[r.ref]={...d.closures[r.ref],completed:true,text,at:now(),author:d.profile.name||'מפעיל מקומי',actorId:tfActor(),doneConfirmed:true,permitChecked:true};d.delays.filter(x=>x.workRef===r.ref&&x.state==='open').forEach(x=>{x.state='resolved';x.resolution='העבודה הושלמה: '+text;x.resolvedAt=now();});d.assignments.filter(a=>a.workRef===r.ref&&a.status!=='cancelled').forEach(a=>{a.status='done';a.updatedAt=now();});const task=d.tasks.find(t=>t.ref===r.ref);if(task){const plan=d.plans.find(p=>p.id===task.planId);if(plan){let next=addDays(task.due,plan.intervalDays);while(next<=today())next=addDays(next,plan.intervalDays);plan.nextDue=next;plan.lastCompleted=today();}}});
}
function savePlan(input,id=''){const title=requireText(input.title,'שם תוכנית',180),asset=requireText(input.asset,'ציוד / מיקום',160),intervalDays=Number(input.intervalDays),duration=Number(input.duration);if(!Number.isInteger(intervalDays)||intervalDays<1||intervalDays>3650)throw Error('תדירות צריכה להיות מספר שלם של ימים, 1–3650.');if(!(duration>0&&duration<=16))throw Error('משך עבודה צריך להיות עד 16 שעות.');if(!validDate(input.nextDue))throw Error('יש לבחור מועד יעד תקין.');const item={id:id||uid('pm'),title,asset,dept:String(input.dept||''),intervalDays,duration,nextDue:input.nextDue,steps:String(input.steps||'').slice(0,3000),active:true};commit(id?'עדכון תוכנית מונעת':'תוכנית מונעת חדשה',item.id,d=>{const i=d.plans.findIndex(x=>x.id===id);if(i>=0)d.plans[i]={...d.plans[i],...item};else d.plans.push(item);});return item;}
function createPlanTask(id){const p=db.plans.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!p||!p.active)throw Error('התוכנית אינה פעילה.');const ref='pm:'+p.id+':'+p.nextDue;const prior=db.tasks.find(x=>x.ref===ref);if(prior)return prior;const t={id:uid('task'),ref,planId:p.id,title:p.title+' · '+p.asset,dept:p.dept,due:p.nextDue,created:today(),steps:p.steps,type:'G2',duration:p.duration};commit('פתיחת משימה מונעת מקומית',ref,d=>d.tasks.push(t));return t;}
function saveReview(input){const r=record(input.workRef);if(!r)throw Error('ההודעה לא נמצאה.');if(!input.dept)throw Error('יש לבחור מחלקת יעד.');commit('סינון בוקר / ניתוב',r.ref,d=>{d.reviews[r.ref]={dept:input.dept,section:String(input.section||'').slice(0,80),note:String(input.note||'').slice(0,1500),at:now(),author:d.profile.name||'מפעיל מקומי'};});}
